2026-10-23T01:04:29.0748366Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Serve the Decode round-2 candidates on image 39875650446 with the pre-serving kernel selection overlapped with the gate and Prefill startup

Pin image 39875650446 (build of 5451914):
- sha256 c2f72e057c98562d10cbf39326d70f6fa2fcec9aef8bdcfac08366dd8cd76500
- 12269404160 bytes
Its host carries the opt-in Decode dense GEMM config and the MegaMoE L2 prefetch template argument. Every other setting is main 3642c64: hot-4 Prefill replication, cap 128, concurrency 192 and EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4.

The Firmus hpcai partition caps a job at 17 minutes, and the last run (25458) took 15:45:
- numeric gate: about 2.5 min;
- READY: after 7.3 min;
- normal benchmark: about 3.6 min;
- profile pass: about 4.7 min.
A serial selection step therefore cannot fit. sglang.sbatch now overlaps it:
- select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh "moe" (Triton router gate GEMM, then MegaMoE L2 prefetch 0/8/16) runs on the head node's other four GPUs while the numeric gate runs. Each probe has a 4-minute timeout.
- "dense" (12 QKV / O candidates: DeepGEMM configs, Triton, FlashInfer) runs on the Decode GPUs of the tail node while the three Prefill workers start. It has a 7-minute timeout, which would push the Decode start by at most about 4.5 minutes past the gate.
- The Decode worker starts after "dense" and sources the concatenated env parts. A probe that fails or times out contributes nothing, so its kernel stays as on main.
- The numeric gate keeps the configured path (--dense-selected with no selection env checks stock only). Each selector checks its own candidates numerically, including changed-input CUDA graph replays, before timing them.

This run has no [profile] tag, because the profile pass would not fit with the selection. Per-kernel evidence comes from results/decode-selection/*.json, which holds every sample, block medians and the selection reason. A follow-up profile can be taken once the selected env is pinned.

The harness test now provides the selection env parts and the background pid that the Decode launch waits for. The full qwen-experiment-metrics suite passes locally.
